Something in that man's room is just...not right. But it's not something that a semi-mortal like her could possibly put a finger on.
The Ghoul casts a worried glance at the road behind her before locking the Lexus and heading inside the fancy hotel. She slides the card through the lock and opens the door. She stands frozen in place, unable to cross over the threshold of the Baali's temporary dwelling space.
Cassandra collects herself and walks into the room proper. For her, it's like walking from a fully air-conditioned room into a dusty, muggy old street. An acidic (or sulfuric, perhaps) smell penetrates her nostrils.
Feeling disoriented and lightheaded, she almost passes out from the stench alone. She stumbles into the bathroom and splashes her face with some water. The mirror is steamed up for some reason, which is odd.
When she wipes it with her hand, a dark and shadowy figure flashes out of view. Blink and you'll miss it sort of thing. She swallows the lump in her throat and gasps, her lower lip shaking with fear.
The room is still in a mess. Regardless, Cassandra has trouble recalling what it was that Carla wanted her to retrieve from Sven's temporary abode.
The Ghoul paces around in odd circles and snaps her fingers a few times, her eyes narrowed in deep concentration. A snarling noise comes from somewhere inside the suite.
Cassandra stops, her body jerking around as she tries to locate the source of the noise.
"Hello? Anyone there? I--I'm a friend of Sven's, alright? I'm just here to get some stuff of his! I--are you a...err...groupie of his or something?"
Cass can feel the weight of a million eyes staring right into her very being. There is a rustling sound coming from under the bed.
Under the bed- that's where the monsters usually hide.
Cassandra vividly recalls her times as a small child, when she'd be deathly afraid of the rumbling noises that she thought she had heard beneath her bed. She'd sleep with the lights on. Her father would come in and even go to sleep on the rocking chair in her room just to ensure that his daughter felt safe.
Cassandra cautiously moves forward, getting down on her knees. She lets out a shaky breath and moves the bed sheets aside.
She draws back, gulping. She sees what appears to be some kind of sigil drawn in blood on the floor underneath the bed. Her mind goes a little numb.
She starts to hyperventilate. The longer that she's in this room, the more the pressure starts to build up. Those millions of unseen eyes now feel like billions. Or maybe trillions? Cassandra's vision blurs and she falls onto her arse.
Cass picks herself up with a light whimper, hastily grabs whatever she can be bothered to recall, and gets the bloody 'ell out of there! She has a tight and pained look on her face as she heads towards the elevator. Sadly, it closes on her face just before she can slip in.
"Ahhh, COME ON!"
Back in room 606, something stirs. Cassandra hears another menacing snarl. She looks over her shoulder and sees an unusually large arm with talons for nails emerge through the door...which is supposed to be locked.
Something foul and menacing dances across whatever counts as the thin arm's "skin". Cassandra tries to process what she's seeing, but her mind flat-out rejects the image of whatever's emerging out from Sven's room.
A nagging feeling inside her gut tells her that this thing...probably isn't remotely close to being on this world. Not even a little bit. Not even an iota.
There's more sinister snarling, followed by smoke rising through the cracks between the door and its frame. This creature is leaking some kind of black ink or tar that starts to seep into the carpeted floor and towards Cassandra's feet.
She tightly clutches the items and runs down the stairs. Her heart rate is through the roof, and she feels like passing out. She isn't aware of just how many flights of stairs she has descended until she is in the lobby. Sweat streaks down her face, her arms and legs feel like jelly, and her breathing is still a tad erratic.
"What the 'ell was that?" the Ghoul fearfully asks herself, wondering if she's even ready to hear the answers to such a query. The rain outside the hotel continues to come down, coming down even harder now.
She breathes out, tosses the items in the backseat, and drives off. While in traffic, Cassandra spares a glance at the items through the rearview mirror.
There's a paperback novel of some kind (with a badly designed cover). It looks cheap and tacky. There's a scarf that's frayed at the edges, a pair of leather gloves that look like someone chewed them up, a small owl made carved out of wood, and what appears to be an odd-looking trinket.
It looks almost like an antique gold badge, but with sharpened edges and some interesting little details on it. It is not warm to the touch, yet something about it just feels...wrong. Off in a way that even Cassandra can't quite explain. She decides to ignore it for now.
Traffic soon picks up. Along the way back to Sven, she stops by the pharmacy to buy that medicine for Carla- the pharmacist shoots her an odd look, his eyebrow quirked up in mild suspicion. Cassandra just forces a smile and goes along with it.
After doing that, she gets the coffee for the mortal. Spanish latte with oat milk, priced at an outrageous £11.20!
Cassandra places the cup in the holder and drives back to the venue. Her window wipers move rhythmically in tandem with the sound of rain pounding on the roof of the Lexus.
By the time, the Toreador Ghoul returns, Sven appears to be wrapping up. Which is odd, considering that last night's concert went on for a whole lot longer. His movements and overall tone of voice both seem off.
But it's not just because the Baali has been channeling a great deal of Vitae into his Presence Discipline. An unspeakable heaviness seems to be weighing down on the bathrobe-wearing rockstar.
Amy's near the front. Cassandra joins her and plants a quick peck on her cheek. The Baali does a little more talking with the audience, leaving everyone in attendance utterly flabbergasted by what they had just witnessed.
Sven clears his throat, runs a hand through his hair, and says, "I'd like to thank the sponsor of this event. C'mon, people, let's give it up for CODACORP! Do you Brits know what a hip-hip-hooray is?!"
Loud roars and clamors of approval erupt from the audience. Even Amy is excited. Cassandra, on the other hand, winces a little. How ironic that CODACORP can sponsor an event of this magnitude but can't even pay their employees a decent wage; her mind goes back to those Brujah in the Horn 'N' Stag pub.
She remembers how baggy-eyed they were. She recalls how they looked at their work overalls with a measure of disdain.
Sven leads the crowd in cheering for the primary sponsor of tonight's event. After that, he abruptly flashes a victory sign and dips, much to the great disappointment of the audience. They all want more of him. More Sven...
The armed guards step forward and create some distance between the audience and the stage, telling people to calm down.
Sven's dead eyes linger on Cassandra, but not in a baleful manner. He looks...disappointed with her. But why? He shakes his head and briskly walks off the stage.
Cassandra breathes out, tension building up in her shoulders. She squeezes Amy's hand and decides to follow the Baali and (hopefully) get some answers as to what's going on in that hotel suite of his.
"Gonna go and get a breath of fresh air, yeah?"
Amy simply nods and watches as Cass takes off.
The Ghoul moves through the backstage staff and follows after the bathrobe-clad rockstar. Clara is nowhere to be seen. When Cass asks around, she's met with shrugs, groans, and noncommittal grunts.
She sees Sven quickly getting inside an SUV with tinted windows. She catches a glimpse of Clara sitting in the backseat and looking a bit afraid. The car doors close, and the Baali rockstar is seemingly gone. The tires screech as the car drives away at full speed.
A lump forms in Cassandra's throat, but she steels herself.
"DAMMIT! FUCK!" a man's harsh voice causes Cassandra to yelp and jump out of her skin.
He appears to be middle-aged and of a short stature. He sports a short and cropped beard that's starting to go gray. His skin is fair and his eyes are bloodshot.
His suit- shirt included- is black in color. He places his hands on his hips and sharply exhales, not sparing the Ghoul next to him a glance.
Cassandra awkwardly says, "Hiya. Are you...paparazzi? You just missed him."
"Do I look like the fucking paparazzi to you?" he rudely asks while glowering at her.
Without waiting for her to give an answer, he pulls out a flip phone and speedily dials a number. He presses the phone to his ear, turns around, and waits.
"It--it's me. Yes. I'm sorry, I--he got--yes. Understood. OK. Of--of course. Yes, yes, fine." With a shaking hand, he closes the phone and hurriedly rushes out of the area.
Cassandra has a really bad feeling about this. She slowly follows after him, almost bumping into someone in the process. She loudly calls out, "Oi!"
Black suit man hurriedly walks off. Cassandra makes a right and calls out again, "Oi!"
He makes a sharp turn and disappears amongst the sea of clueless groupies and dazed hangers-on. Sven left without shagging them. How inconsiderate!
Many of them are left wondering if their musical idol (and perhaps something more) will come back and do some "schmoozing". There are some vaguely Scottish accents tossed in with the mix of people.
Cassandra paces around the back areas and spots a familiar...head of hair in the slowly declining throng of people. Black suit man is nowhere to be seen.
Marble Cake, the Brujah employee of CODACORP, on the other hand is present. She's dressed in a cobbled together outfit, wearing a fleece vest over a torn half-sleeve shirt. Her checkered skirt flows down to her ankles, and there are some cheap anklets wrapped around her ankles.
A somewhat dejected look adorns her pale features as she prepares to strut out of this place. Her contrasting brown and blonde hair is covered by a beanie with ear flaps. She approaches 1 of the nearby guards, the scowl on her face turning into an awkward smile.
Cassandra calls out, "Marble Cake!"
The Punk turns around, her brows knit together in mild confusion, "What? Who?"
Cassandra earnestly waves out. The Brujah walks over and musters up a smile.
The Ghoul asks, "Didn't expect to see you here, yeah? Is this your kind of music?"
Marble Cake sighs and shrugs indifferently, "Not really. I only popped by because CODACORP employees are entitled to a 15% discount for the most basic tickets. Since we're all nearing poverty wages, I reckoned that I'd drop by and see what our 'skint' employers were blowing their cash on."
She snickers and adds, "Didn't expect him to abruptly leave us hanging. Americans, right? What are you doing here, anyway?"
Cass evasively responds, "Helping out a bit, I suppose. How's Harry?"
"'Arry's doing fine. Same old, same old. Even though he's glad to see the back of your Domitor, he asked about you and, well..."
She takes a moment to properly recall a conversation with her fellow Rebel, "He says that he hopes you're doing well, all things considered."
Cassandra swallows the lump in her throat and smiles, "That's very kind of him. Are you--are you lot spreading stories about what I did--"
Marble Cake chuckles, "We sure are. But in Kindred Society, it's like a game of telephone. Why? You don't like the new reputation that's being built up for you?"
"It's not that. I just did what I had to do, yeah? I--I'm not exactly proud of it."
"Relax, love. What you did doesn't necessarily count as murder- Winston was long gone by the time you brought Final Death upon him. You DO know how Wights work, right? Frenzying--"
"I--I know all that, yeah. I 'ave to get going. Give Harry and the rest of your mates me best, alright?"
Marble Cake nods and grins, "Take care, Cassandra. And be mindful out there, yeah? Be watchful, too." There is a hint of concern in the Punk's tone.
"Y--yeah."
They part ways, with Cassandra lightly squeezing past some people and approaching a random guard. She asks if he knows what Sven's schedule might be or where he may be heading.
He denies having any such information and tells her to beat it.

The mortal pulls Cassandra in for another round of passionate smooching and heavy petting. Tongues clash up against each other. Amy lets out a moan when Cassandra goes down and lightly bites her neck.

"She has her dalliances too. Isn't that what you once said??"
Amy shakes her head, "No. For all her...flaws, she...has been faithful. Maybe I--I ought to--never mind. I have to go back home now, OK? Please do not text me at odd hours of the night, promise?"
Cassandra wipes the tear away from her eye and sniffles, "Fine. Promise. But we should...get together again, yeah? I enjoyed meself--"
Amy replies, "And I did, too. But we have to pace ourselves out. Goodnight, babe." She blows a kiss, winks, turns on her heel, and starts walking off. While walking away, she puts her ring back on. A look of guilt flashes across her face for a split second before disappearing.
Cassandra gathers her composure and walks in a separate direction. Before she's about to push the exit door open, black suit man accosts her and holds something against her back. It feels round and metallic.
Her stomach drops and her heart is racing.
The guy gruffly says, "Walk straight ahead. There's someone who needs to speak with you...and your Domitor. You're--you're Gregory's Ghoul, aren't you?"
Cassandra gasps, silently wondering to himself how this strange man knows so much. She stiffens up and doesn't say a word.
"Hrrrfh. Fine. 'Ave it your way, then. Don't you dare pull a runner on me like that fucking Demon Worshipper, you hear?"

She fearfully nods her head up and down.
"Good. Good. Someone is already picking Gregory up. Somebody very important wishes to speak to both of you."

"You'll see soon enough, luv. You'll see soon enough. No use in me spoiling the surprise."
With her hands held up, she is ordered to get into an unremarkable sedan that is parked nearby. Cassandra goes in slowly, followed by black suit man. He doesn't take his eyes off of her for even a second.
He starts the car up and peels out of the venue.
What does this wanker want? And how the 'ell does he know so much?
"Do you mind at least telling me WHERE you're taking me?" Cassandra asks, exhibiting a bit of firmness in her voice that wasn't wholly present beforehand.
Black suit man, who never bothered to put his seat belt on, replies, "No funny business."
For the remainder of the tense ride, he says nothing else. He does give her a look to ensure that her hands are visible at all times.
When they get to their destination, Cassandra blinks and looks outside the water-streaked window. Her mouth hangs agape as she sees an unremarkable office building of some kind. It's only 2 stories tall and connected by an elevated walkway.
The walls are dull, with the light brown paint peeling off. The glass windows have some dust on them and look like they haven't been cleaned in years.
"What is this place?"
Black suit man uses his silenced gun to prod her out of the vehicle. Cassandra obeys, stepping outside and shivering from the cool air hitting her body. With an umbrella in the other hand, he escorts her through a glass door and into an equally bland and underwhelming building.
Their feet scrape against some aged linoleum flooring. The walls are a bland shade of green, with the flickering fluorescent lights providing a touch of illumination. An unpleasant and earthy smell lingers in the air.
They both head to the topmost floor and briskly walk to the farthest room to their right. Black suit man holds the gun up to Cassandra's back while he uses the keycard on his person to unlock the door.
He shoves her inside and orders, "Wait here. No funny business, OK?"
The Ghoul merely nods her head and complies, standing there as still as she can possibly be. She looks around and notes 2 folding metal chairs, a plush maroon office chair, and a somewhat rusted metal table separating the 2 chairs from the other one.
The room is seemingly colder than the hallway itself. Cassandra hugs herself and shivers a bit.
"Brrrr! Blimey!"

Cassandra's voice goes up a few octaves, "Gregory! What's going on here?!"

Standing at the doorway is a tall and somewhat well-built woman in her early-40s. She has alabaster skin, styled blonde hair, a long neck, and shiny gray eyes. She sports a puff-sleeved blouse, high-waisted pants that hug her wide hips, and suede ankle boots.

She is Welsh by birth, but her accent is plain, generic, and decidedly British. She clasps her cold hands together and regards the 2 individuals seated in front of her with a look that would put even the sternest of schoolmarms to shame.

After locking the vehicle up, she trudges into her cottage and plops down onto the couch. Cassandra tries to relax, but sleep eludes her at the moment.
She's slowly starting to realize what eternal servitude in exchange for eternal life (or a variant thereof) actually entails. She'll need some time to process it, though.
But she's too deep in it to back out. To reject the Blood of her Domitor would prove to be nothing but fatal. The rapid aging alone would send the Ghoul into shock, assuming that she even survives it at all.
So, in other words, there is no going back for her.
Cassandra spends a few minutes watching whatever the hell's on ITV at this odd hour of the night. When her eyelids grow heavy, she lets out a tired sigh and sluggishly makes her way to bed. She flops facedown onto the covers.
Sleep overtakes her in mere seconds.
Cassandra's snores are slightly muffled. A gentle breeze wafts through her partially open windows, filling her room with the soothing smells of damp earth and grass.
Suddenly, the Toreador Ghoul's eyes snap open. She's standing in a vast and empty space that stretches on for as far as her eyes can see. Her feet aren't touching anything resembling solid ground. A floaty feeling fills her body. It's a strange sensation to say the least.
A familiar sigil of blood begins to draw itself on a wall that popped out of nowhere- it's the same exact sigil seen under the bed in the rockstar's hotel suite. Cassandra blinks a few times. When her eyes readjust to her surroundings, she spots a familiar form coming towards her.
Sven runs a hand through his hair. He's shirtless, proudly displaying his washboard abs and smooth chest for all the world to see. Or Cassandra, as it were.
Cassandra tries to speak, to move, to blink, but it feels as if she's suddenly trapped within the confines of whatever this space is.
Or wherever it is.
The Baali stops a few meters away from her and raises his chin, "So, they found me out, huh? They're onto me now? Shame, I was at least hoping on getting through the week before being forced into hiding again. I hated having to go underground for years on end."
He pauses and smiles at her in a way that is anything but reassuring. His voice is low and soothing when he says, "Don't worry, though. We're in this together. Did you really think that there'd be no consequences for you stepping into my dark sanctum- temporary as it may've been?"
Cassandra can only watch. Sven, on the other hand, is free to roam around this place.
The Baali resumes, "You stepped into my temp Haven- twice! Now, I get a free pass into your mind, girlie. I'm still getting acquainted with this joint. Cassandra's head. The branding opportunities alone..." He trails off. He's enjoying this immensely.
Cass helplessly watches as he paces around her, his footsteps making no sounds on the "ground" below.
"Lemme show you something real quick, OK?" He directs the Ghoul's attention to a distorted image in the distance.
Black suit man- the guy from earlier- is seen entering room 606. A scream is heard but the sound is horribly distorted and warped. Something liquefied seeps through the cracks and under the ajar door. It's partially red in color.
Sven winces and shakes his head, "You should be lucky my...pals didn't do that to you when you went snooping around my suite. Whatever was Clara thinking, huh? For what it's worth, I...uh...I'm sorry if whatever- or whoever- you saw in there caused you any undue stress or trauma or...whatever."
The Baali seems to mean it.
His tone shifts to something a little more sinister, "Still...you should never have entered. And twice! Don't worry, I'll sort Clara out. Not in THAT way, of course. I'm not a--a fuckin' animal!" He laughs heartily and wipes away a bloody tear.
Cassandra's fingers twitch a little.
Sven playfully shoves her. In this trippy space, her body slowly leaves a trail as it is pushed away. She's able to turn her head, though, so that she is looking right into Sven's dead eyes.
"I'm still learning all that there is to about you, Cass. Cassie. So, my pal Gregory has you running around doing...errands on his behalf, huh? Not a bad gig, all things considered. Hey, at least you get a vacation from work for...weeks. Wow. Nice."
He's slowly learning everything. But how did he even manage to intrude her psyche and enter her mind at all? These are the questions that run through Cass's mind right now.
Sven waggles a finger at her, "Ah. Of course. Lemme explain it from the top. Wow, for a Ghoul, you do seem pretty damn knowledgeable about our kind. Most of 'em just wanna fuck, fight, and party all day. But you have an idea of what you got yourself into, huh? Mm-hmm."
He explains, "As Baali, our Bloodline has this Compulsion to Compact with...a Daimonion. A Daimonion. Singular."
But Sven over here got greedy and decided to go for a whole goddamn host of them...including some creatures from the Umbra.
The heartthrob shudders and looks away, "It happened and it can't be taken back now. Heh. Wherever I go, I drag a whole uh...village of entities around with me. Even they need a place to stay. You do know where this is headed, right? Right?"
He fixes her with a look of concern and shrugs, "Hm. OK. And when you stepped into room 606, you kinda set off a couple of alarm bells. Sleeping horrors DO NOT appreciate being disturbed. And if they do, they usually tend to take it out on me. So...yikes."
The Baali points a finger gun at her head and pulls the imaginary trigger, "Boom! Well...nothing so dramatic, haha! But you get the point, right? I'm stuck with 'em forever. And ever and ever and ever. Until fucking Gehenna comes knocking."
Sven snaps his fingers and a doorway appears. Behind it is an abyss of pure nothingness. It looks like it stretches on for infinity, but it also feels like it has no bottom to speak of.
He puts his hand on the terrified Ghoul's shoulder and presses his lips into a thin line, "I can't go back now. I've become addicted to--to the success, the high, the attention, the money, the--the adoration of the crowds, the swooning of the masses, and the afterparties with the groupies."
Sven looks over his shoulder and speaks a little faster, "I can't turn back now. And I--I won't. They won't catch me. And you can tell that hot Tzimisce chick...everything. Like it even makes a fuckin' difference, if I'm being totally honest with ya. And I do intend to be upfront with you. Completely."
The Unholy Kindred grimly concludes, "And we are gonna be seeing each other from now. NOT in that way, of course. You're an intriguing woman, Cassie. I'm gonna poke around this head of yours, see what I can find, and we're gonna have some nice conversations here and there. Don't worry, I'll tune out the...naughty stuff, alright?"
He gives her a playful nudge on the cheek and retracts his hand, "And we'll talk. I won't tell you where I am because I know that you'll just go and blurt it all out to whomever. But I'm inside your head now, so you better get used to me. Alright? Alright."
Before Sven vanishes, he raises a finger, "Those sons of bitches grounded my private jet and killed my pilots. Ah, well, I guess I'm stuck in damp country for the time being. And the monsters under your bed are very real, Cassandra. Very real. Not even your dad sitting opposite your bed on his rocking chair could've saved you from them."
He waves his hand and abruptly disappears, "Ta-ta! Bye-bye!"
Numerous thin hands with talons for fingers begin to pour out of the doorway to nowhere. A chorus of terrifying whispers fill the space. A tidal wave of blackened blood splashes over Cassandra and sends her tumbling through this weird space.
Cassandra wakes up with a horrible wheeze. Her body jerks up from the mattress. The sun now shines through the open windows of her bedroom. There's no sign of an intruder- nobody else in the room save for the Toreador Ghoul herself.
Act 5: Children Of Osiris
"The Osirians aren't a Bloodline in the strictest sense of the word. They are known to accept members of differing Clans and Sects- save for 1. They view themselves as the solution to the Setites. And yes, they have asked me to join. No, I am...comfortable where I am. But I do appreciate the sentiment, nonetheless. They seem to be decent creatures. We get along well and I have even attended some of their ceremonies in the past." -From the personal audio recordings of Gregory | Entry: On The Children Of Osiris.
A new day dawns over Gloughchester. Birds sing their morning songs. The sky is a clear, azure blue with a few clouds in sight.
Cassandra takes a few moments to collect herself before hopping off the bed and going about her daily routine. Disorientation still lingers in her head for a bit but she's able to recover from last night's dreamscape accosting.
She clutches her head and stares at herself in the mirror, "Cor blimey. Am I a--a sight for sore eyes, yeah."
The still-frightened Ghoul squeezes out some toothpaste onto her toothbrush and sets about cleaning her teeth. She gargles some salt water, spits it out, and takes a few more moments to stare at the messy-haired lass looking back at her from the other side of the mirror.
Cass trills her lips and sighs, "Nothing I can't handle. Nothing I can't handle."
After bathing, she steps out of the tub, sopping wet. She slips and almost falls backwards into the basin but manages to catch herself just in time. She lets out a tired huff.
"Bloody 'ell!"
The remainder of the day passes by in a blur. Cassandra swings by the ASDA supermarket, buys some things so she can make a nice home-cooked lunch, and then heads back to her cottage to cook the food up.
She shoots a few texts to Amy while the chopped vegetables are boiling in a pot. Amy texts back and the both of them get to chatting. Time flies by. Cassandra rushes around the kitchen, stirring something else while also checking to see if the food's done yet. The delicious smell fills the air.
Cassandra inhales a lungful of it and sighs contently, "Mm-mmm. Smells bloody scrumptious, innit?"
After taking her lunch, she puts the leftovers in some plastic containers and keeps them in the fridge. From there, she ambles around for a few minutes. By 4 in the afternoon, she decides to take a light nap.
However, an uneasy feeling keeps her awake. It nags at her, asking if Sven will pop out of nowhere again. She glances at the time on her Blackberry phone. The Ghoul lets out a shaky sigh. Who will she tell about this? And she has to tell somebody!
She gulps and feels her stomach churning a bit. Cass takes a deep breath and then exhales.
By the time sleep takes a hold of her, she elects to tell Gregory and Sheriff Alice about this. The former will, hopefully, be understanding. The latter, on the other hand, might not be.
Cassandra soon eases into a restful slumber. The sound of birds chirping fills her ears. The gentle pitter-patter of rain, the soothing smell of the damp earth and grass, and the calming sound of the breeze caressing the trees and blowing through her window brings a certain relief to the napping retainer.
Oh, dear.
Cass is pulled back into the same place, same thing. Sven spreads his arms to either side of his muscular bod and cries out, "Welcome back, Cassie! So, I've been doing some non-invasive prying of your...mind, memories, and personality. You're a pretty fuckin' interesting chick. That's a compliment!"
The Baali spares her a pitiful glance, "Ah, sorry. Hey, for what it's worth, I promise not to perv around or--or creepily creep you out. Believe it or not, but I know boundaries. And I sure as fuck know consent, too.
He chuckles, "And go on. Tell the Sheriff about me. I won't divulge my escape plan to ya- I'm still trying to figure it all out, hahaha! Oh, and by the way..."
Sven suddenly flashes next to her. He leans in closer and ominously whispers, "My real name's not Sven..."
Cassandra is jolted awake once more. The winds outside, the sounds of nature, and the smells permeate her cottage. She wheezes, her chest rising and falling at a quicker pace. She hugs her knees tightly, her eyes darting around the room.
Once she has calmed down, she scrambles through her wardrobe for an outfit. For tonight, it's some Burberry slacks, a plain white blouse, and some rather stylish white loafers. She adds some bangles and jingling earrings on for good measure. A necklace featuring a red gemstone decorates her neck.
Cassandra downs a whole glass of water, wipes her mouth with the back of her hand, and proceeds to do her makeup. Simple pound shop lip gloss and some moisturizer. A touch of green rouge, nothing too fancy.
A green headband secures the top of her hair. She sprays some rose-scented perfumes that Amy had bought for her. Cassandra steps away from the mirror and examines herself.
She struggles to muster a smile. She simply pats the sides of her legs and sighs feebly.
Oddly enough, the Aapilu didn't haunt Gregory prior to his awakening from the day-sleep. It's worse, actually. The creature of the Vitae merely lingers in the darker corners of the Toreador's Haven, watching him in a literal, crawling manner. Cautiously. The thing is slinking around like a patch of icky darkness.
It tilts its head and crouches while Gregory combs his hair. His cold hands are trembling a little. He forces a deep breath to calm himself. The reflection in his mirror reveals a nervous-looking Domitor.
Greg sees the Aapilu from the corner of his eye but can't quite bring himself to look at it directly, let alone confront it. He lifts his chin and marches downstairs to the sitting room. After a minute, Cassandra knocks on his glass door and asks to be let in.
Gregory ushers her in. He notices that she's not her usual bouncy self. However, he doesn't inquire any further. Instead, he opts to sit in his comfy armchair and resume reading a book.
Cassandra squints but can't quite make out what her Domitor's reading. She clears her throat and the other Kindred turns another page, still not looking up.
"Greg..."
The Aesthete sighs and calmly sets the book down on the glass table next to his chair, "Yes?"
He looks up and studies her with a steady gaze, "How are you holding up, Cassandra?"
The Ghoul sharply exhales and plops herself down on the couch, "I'm...I'm fine, yeah. You?"
"Likewise. Are you ready for tonight's undertaking- this one shan't necessarily be dangerous, but caution is advised all the same." Gregory loosens his stiff collar a little bit.
Cassandra clasps her warm hands together and vacantly stares at the ground, "We'll see. I--I have to tell you something. It's about your Baali mate--"
"He is not my 'mate'. Well, not anymore. I do not willingly associate with the Sabbat. Had I perhaps known of his true allegiances, then..." Gregory shakes his head and grunts.
"I saw him."
"What do you mean by that?"
Cassandra steadies herself and tries her best to explain her current predicament, "When--when I was sleeping, he appeared to me. Or...took me somewhere. I--I don't know. It's a bit...hard to explain. But every time I sleep, he told me to expect him."
Gregory nods and steeples his fingers, "Hm. This is a rather disturbing development. And Sven warned you not to breathe a word of this to anyone, didn't he?"
Cass weakly chuckles, "It doesn't matter. He sees what I see. Somehow, he even knows about the Sheriff and the fact that he's wanted."
"Oh. I see. Do you feel comfortable with informing Sheriff Alice, then?"
"I don't 'ave much of a choice, Greg. W--will you come with me?" The pleading is fairly evident in her hazel eyes.
Gregory absentmindedly closes his book and plainly nods, "If that is what you wish. But before we take our leave, I need to bring up a few things."
"Hmph. Yeah. Children of Osiris. I haven't forgotten. So, they owe you? Mind if I ask what exactly for? What kind of a favor are we talking here?" She sits a little straighter and lifts her chin to listen.
The Toreador answers, "This one won't be as risky as you're expecting, worry not. I simply need you to hand over some...goods to the Osirians for transport to locales far and wide. That is all, nothing more."
The retainer lets out a sigh of relief. She relaxes a bit and musters up a look of gratitude, "That's...that's good to hear, yeah. Moving stuff all over the world. What kind of stuff, Greg?"
Gregory rises from his seating and beckons for Cassandra to follow him. While walking to the downstairs basement, he further explains, "These items are mainly symbolic pieces. Sacred, some might say. Decorative, perhaps. Most are intended for Egypt, but some for other lands."
Gregory stops and stares at the fortified door. It's very shiny and made of thick metal. The secure locks can only be operated via 2 passcodes, 1 of which he possesses and the other is committed to Cassandra's memory.
They take their respective positions and enter the code. After a blue light flashes, the thick door hisses open. The Artisan politely gestures for his Ghoul to enter.
Cassandra's eyebrows shoot up as she steps into the cool basement vault. Scores upon scores of art pieces, sculptures, vases, figurines, and art-adjacent objects are neatly organized against the walls.
Every time she enters in here, she is hit by this feeling of awe that hasn't worn off since the first time. It's like a mini art gallery but super awesome. But there is a bit of guilt lurking deep inside her heart. Why are these items just cooped up here in a basement?
Moreover, many of these pieces weren't exactly acquired through wholly ethical means. And a lot of them do actually belong in a public museum for all to see. Not locked up, hidden away, and kept a secret from the general populace.
Gregory has grown rather indifferent over the long years in dealing with, trafficking, and moving such objects of immense value (NOT strictly monetary). One does what one must to survive, even as an immortal predator of the nocturnal hours.
With purpose punctuating his each and every step, Gregory gently walks over to a golden bust and adjusts its position. He runs his pale, long fingers along the smooth texture of the object.
His dead blue eyes linger over the way it shines under the stark white lights above. His lower lip quivers when the world around him goes blurry. His hearing gets a tad muffled.
All he can focus on- at this moment- is the sheen of the golden artifact. The exquisite details. Every single crevice and nook. The impressive manner in which the imperfections are almost entirely unnoticeable.
That's the Toreador Clan Compulsion playing up. Their Obsession renders them unable to take their attention from whatever (or whoever) enraptures them at the moment. And, admittedly, this particular piece of antiquity is quite the stunner!
Cassandra folds her arms and silently watches along; by now, she has gotten quite used to her Domitor's foibles. She tries to follow his gaze so that she too may be enthralled by whatever's gotten her master all stirred up.
Gregory eventually blinks and regains control of his faculties. Yet again, he reaches out and adjusts the position of the bust. He clicks his tongue in mild annoyance, pulls out his large handkerchief, and gingerly wipes the object's surface clean.
He does that 1 more time before moving on, satisfied with the current placement of the golden bust.
Cassandra stifles a chuckle and casually comments, "Wanna talk about it?"
"I--I'm fine, thank you." He turns his head away and gestures to a sizeable wooden crate in the corner of the vault. He lifts the lid and picks up a chunky-looking scarab that's about the size of a child's clenched fist. It's smooth all over. The outer layer feels like it's been polished by expert hands.
Cass inquires, "What exactly is that?"
Gregory inspects it carefully, "It is rumored that the Blood of Set the Antediluvian is held within this particular artifact. Many have fruitlessly attempted to break through the shell in hopes of acquiring such an elusive prize. None have succeeded. I have personally witnessed someone attempting to utilize a diamond drill on this."
He brings it closer to Cassandra's face and traces the scratches that the aforementioned activity left behind, "All they did was mar the beauty of it. Tch. Tasteless savages. They wanted to blow it open, but I managed to convince them not to."
Cassandra pulls back and purses her lips, "Right. What else is in 'ere?" She leans closer to the box and noses about.
The Toreador nods, "Aside from an assortment of pieces acquired over the years, the rest are trinkets. There's even an ancient tome on hieroglyphics. I secured it at an auction for £10,000. And this was long before we even met."
Cassandra exclaims, "Cor blimey! £10,000?! What's that in today's quid?"
"A rather sizable amount, I should imagine."
"Yeah, probably enough to feed a family for a--a few months, maybe?"
"Hm. Yes. Perhaps. Now, is your Lexus clean?"
A little later...
Cassandra is seated across Sheriff Alice. Gregory waits outside this time. Same place as last night. Same room, same seating arrangements, too.
The Tzimisce Sheriff has a hard look on her face. There's a sense of alertness emanating from her. She raises her chin and silently listens to Cassandra's haphazard explanations. The wild gesticulating doesn't exactly help.

"N--not exactly, no. He's haunting me--me dreams, more like. I think. Maybe I'll ask him for clarity. Hard to wrap me head 'round it all, innit?"
"Yes, one can only imagine. Relay a message to this Infernalist, if you would be so kind."
Cass nods and licks her lips.
Sheriff Alice reaches into a shoulder holster concealed underneath her suit and produces a pistol that looks like something out of a bloody sci-fi flick. It's sleek, shiny, heavy to hold, and has a scope attached to the top of it.
The Stoker slides her hand canon across the table and presents it to Cassandra. She casually elaborates, "Modified to my specifications. If a Ghoul like you tried to fire it, not even the A&E would be able to bring you back. And mortals would likely lose a vital body part owing to the recoil alone."
Cassandra nervously gulps, "W--why are you telling me all this? What do you want me to tell him?"
"If he sees what you see, then..." The Tzimisce points her gnarly hand canon straight at Cass's forehead and continues, "Let him see what awaits him once he is found. For make no mistake, we shall find him...and destroy him without hesitation."

"Y--you weren't going to--to shoot me, were you?"
"Have you done anything to warrant such a harsh penalty? No. Keep being an exemplary citizen and I won't have to resort to any violent solutions."
The Sheriff puts in, "And yes, my firearm is very much capable of inflicting Final Death upon Kindred. As for the...others who have been on its receiving end, the term 'mutilated' wouldn't even begin to do their mushy remains justice."
Cassandra's eyes widen to an alarming degree.
Sheriff Alice's expression softens, "I don't say these things to frighten you or to gloat. No. I have to remind you that the Sabbat are monsters. Extremists. The presence- and confirmation- of 1 in my Domain is reason enough for such measures. Drastic as they may seem, they are necessary all the same."

Sheriff Alice chuckles slowly, "That's very reassuring. And what will an upright citizen like yourself be doing tonight, hmm?"
Cassandra shrugs, "Helping Greg out. He--"
The Fiend raises a stern finger, "You needn't say more, Ghoul. Do what must be done to uphold the Masquerade- it's a fragile thing in the age of surveillance cameras, smartphones, social media, and the like. Remember this: we aren't the only ones hunting this Infernalist."
"Oh? W--who else is hunting him?"
Sheriff Alice's expression turns far grimmer, "I'm afraid that I cannot divulge such information to a lowly Ghoul such as yourself. If you need to be brought into the loop, so to speak, then you will be brought in. Otherwise, just go about your business as normal."
